function openMenuVehicle() {

$('.popin-vehicle-header-without-v').slideToggle().toggleClass('open');

if($('.popin-vehicle-header').hasClass('open')){
$('.overlay-header').addClass('overlay-header-visible');
$('.overlay-header-visible').css('top', 125 + $("#privacy").height() +'px');
} else {
$('.overlay-header').removeClass('overlay-header-visible');
}
if($('.popin-vehicle-header .line-vehicle:first-child').hasClass('checked')){
$('.popin-vehicle-header').addClass('firstLineChecked');
}
}
$(document).ready(function(){
$('.overlay-header').click(function() {
$('.popin-vehicle-header').slideUp().removeClass('open');
});
});

<script>
(function (publicKey) {
woosmapRecommendation.setProjectKey(publicKey);
woosmapRecommendation.getUserRecommendation({
successCallback: function (stores) {
var featuresExist = stores && stores.features && stores.features.length > 0;
var idOk = false;
if(featuresExist){
var storeId = stores.features[0].properties.store_id.split('_')[1];
idOk = storeId !== null && storeId !== undefined && storeId !== '' && !isNaN(storeId);
}
if (idOk) {
var id = storeId;
$.ajax({
url: 'https://www.norauto.pl/INTERSHOP/web/WFS/NI-NOPL-Site/pl_PL/-/PLN/IncludeAjaxMountingCenter-RefreshDivision?DivisionID=' + id,
type: 'GET',
dataType: "html",
traditional: true,
success: function (data) {
$('.center-header-link').addClass('active');
$('#update-division').html(data);
woosmapRecommendation.sendUserHtml5Position({
lat: stores.features[0].geometry.coordinates[1],
lng: stores.features[0].geometry.coordinates[0]
}); 
if (typeof Geoloc !== 'undefined') {
Geoloc.initGeolocUpdate('https://www.norauto.pl/INTERSHOP/web/WFS/NI-NOPL-Site/pl_PL/-/PLN/IncludeAjaxProduct-Start', id, false);
} 
}
});
digitalData.saved_store_woosmap = stores.features[0].properties.address.city + '|' + id;
localStorage.setItem('saved_store_woosmap', stores.features[0].properties.address.city + '|' + id);
} else {
if (window.NodeList && !NodeList.prototype.forEach) {
NodeList.prototype.forEach = function (callback, thisArg) {
thisArg = thisArg || window;
for (var i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
callback.call(thisArg, this[i], i, this);
}
};
}
